=>\n512\t    run(process.execPath, [LOOP], { cwd: CORE, input: JSON.stringify({ tool_name: tool, tool_input: { command } }), env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: home() } });\n513\t  const deny = (r) => {\n514\t    try {\n515\t      return JSON.parse(r.stdout).hookSpecificOutput.permissionDecision === 'deny';\n516\t    } catch {\n517\t      return false;\n518\t    }\n519\t  };\n520\t  const forever = call('Bash', 'until grep -qE \"passed|failed\" out.txt; do sleep 5; done; tail -4 out.txt');\n521\t  ok('an until loop with no bound is denied', forever.status === 0 && deny(forever), forever.stdout);\n522\t  ok('the denial explains how to bound it', /timeout/.test(forever.stdout), forever.stdout);\n523\t  ok('a while true loop is denied too', deny(call('Bash', 'while true; do sleep 10; done')));\n524\t  ok('a PowerShell wait loop is denied', deny(call('PowerShell', 'while (-not (Test-Path out.txt)) { Start-Sleep 5 }')));\n525\t  ok('timeout makes it pass', !deny(call('Bash', 'timeout 5400 bash -c \"until grep -q passed out.txt; do sleep 5; done\"')));\n526\t  ok('a counter makes it pass', !deny(call('Bash', 'i=0; until grep -q passed out.txt || [ $i -ge 60 ]; do sleep 5; i=$((i+1)); done')));\n527\t  ok('a Get-Date deadline makes it pass', !deny(call('PowerShell', '$end = (Get-Date).AddMinutes(90); while ((Get-Date) -lt $end -and -not (Test-Path     if (err.code !== 'ENOENT') throw err;\n66\t    }\n67\t  }\n68\t}\n69\t\n70\tfunction home() {\n71\t  const h = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'tkc-home-'));\n72\t  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(h, 'teknesyum'), { recursive: true });\n73\t  return h;\n74\t}\n75\t\n76\tfunction sweep(dir) {\n77\t  try {\n78\t    fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 3 });\n79\t  } catch {}\n80\t}\n81\t\n82\tfunction hook(script, j, cfg) {\n83\t  return run(process.execPath, [script], {\n84\t    cwd: j.cwd || process.cwd(),\n85\t    input: JSON.stringify(j),\n86\t    env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: cfg, NO_COLOR: '1' },\n87\t  });\n88\t}\n89\t\n90\tfunction edit(root, cfg, rel, sid, text) {\n91\t  const file = path.join(root, rel);\n92\t  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });\n93\t  if (text !== undefined) fs.writeFileSync(file, text);\n94\t  return hook(COUNT, { hook_event_name: 'PostToolUse', tool_name: 'Edit', session_id: sid || 's1', cwd: root, tool_input: { file_path: file } }, cfg);\n95\t}\n96\t\n97\tfunction take(cfg, sid) {\n98\t  const f = path.join(cfg, 'teknesyum', 'banner-' + (sid || 'none') +
